//LINE 1 "/Users/oberon/projects/NewBCPL/test_routine_call.bcl"
LET START() BE
$(
   WRITES("Testing routine calls with variable arguments...*N")

   LET X = 42
   LET Y = 100

   WRITES("Before routine call: X=")
   WRITEN(X)
   WRITES(", Y=")
   WRITEN(Y)
   WRITES("*N")

   WRITES("Calling TEST_ROUTINE with X and Y...*N")
   TEST_ROUTINE(X, Y)

   WRITES("After routine call: X=")
   WRITEN(X)
   WRITES(", Y=")
   WRITEN(Y)
   WRITES("*N")

   WRITES("Testing with string variables...*N")
   LET S1 = "hello"
   LET S2 = "world"

   WRITES("Before string routine: S1=")
   WRITES(S1)
   WRITES(", S2=")
   WRITES(S2)
   WRITES("*N")

   STRING_ROUTINE(S1, S2)

   WRITES("After string routine completed*N")
$)

LET TEST_ROUTINE(A, B) BE
$(
   WRITES("Inside TEST_ROUTINE: A=")
   WRITEN(A)
   WRITES(", B=")
   WRITEN(B)
   WRITES("*N")

   WRITES("Calling WRITES with A and B values...*N")
   WRITES("A + B = ")
   WRITEN(A + B)
   WRITES("*N")
$)

LET STRING_ROUTINE(STR1, STR2) BE
$(
   WRITES("Inside STRING_ROUTINE: STR1=")
   WRITES(STR1)
   WRITES(", STR2=")
   WRITES(STR2)
   WRITES("*N")

   WRITES("Concatenated: ")
   WRITES(STR1)
   WRITES(" ")
   WRITES(STR2)
   WRITES("*N")
$)
